## Manual review checklist

- Verify every source link and source date before external use.
- Confirm image rights; store and share URLs only unless permission is granted.
- Separate directly sourced facts from design interpretation.
- Check that AI/editorial summaries do not overstate designer intent.
- Avoid paywalled, leaked, or unverifiable material.
